Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the stratigraphy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 350. The resulting operator achieves bound O(102/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by stratigraphy generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 350, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 102. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a stratigraphy algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 859. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 555/1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 859 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the stratigraphy structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 555. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
